# Done queue items — archived from docs/OPEN.md (2026-11)

Historical record, not a work list. Every item here was ticked [x] in
docs/OPEN.md and was moved verbatim by `node scripts/archive-done.mjs --write`
(run by `npm run inventories:refresh`; docs/OPEN.md Q16). The queue tools still
read this file with docs/OPEN.md: the score line and next free number
(scripts/queue-count.mjs), the Everything-open block (scripts/scoreboard.mjs)
and src/test/queueItemsNameTheirGuard.test.ts. To REOPEN an item, move its
block back into docs/OPEN.md and untick it (a number in both files fails the
duplicate-number check).

## Archived 2026-11-01 — from "QUEUE (cont.) — carried; the retired audit-bus section's table (2026-09-23) is superseded by the FEEDS mirror above"
- [x] **Q2006 LOW process-scheduled-payouts re-drives held tips and held chargeback clawback repays on the LIVE key for every row, whatever the job's Stripe mode (lh-money-escrow review of the tip fixture, 2026-10-17; code read).** supabase/functions/process-scheduled-payouts/index.ts keeps `stripes.clientFor("live")` (:75-80) only for redriveHeldTips and redriveHeldClawbackRepays (:1993-1999); heldTipRepay.ts repayOne calls transfers.create({destination}) on it (:329-352), and chargebackClawback.ts:850 does the same. A row written by a TEST-mode tip or clawback (newly reachable once helper-e2e holds its sanctioned test-mode account, Q2007, and is on a payout hold) is refused by Stripe: no money moves, but an owed row records a defect every hourly run, and a reversed row goes to failed with a CRITICAL "pay it by hand" page. Measured 2026-10-17: tip_hold_redrives 0 rows, payout_holds 0 rows, so not reachable today. Fix: resolve each row's client with stripes.clientForParties([row.helper_id]) (or skip and log a test-mode row under the live key), with an edge test that fails on the live-only client. finding: lh-money-escrow@9a2c1e875#1 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no commit on origin/main names it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **CLOSED 2026-11-01 (B88): already fixed on main by df1ad99ae7 (Q1753, 2026-10-14, lh-money-escrow approve), which these triages missed.** process-scheduled-payouts passes redriveStripeFor (stripes.clientForJob(job, "payout", payees)) to both redriveHeldClawbackRepays (stripeForJob, per dispute) and redriveHeldTips (stripeForRow, resolved from the tip's job before the claim); the live `stripe` is only the type fallback. Guard: src/test/edge/cronStripeModePerJob.test.ts, 14/14 green on origin/main 84f702c121+; with redriveStripeFor planted as the live-only client, 6 of its cases FAIL (test-job tip and clawback re-pays, a mixed dispute, a real job owed to a test Helpr, a stale owed test tip).

## Archived 2026-11-02 — from "QUEUE (cont.) — carried; the retired audit-bus section's table (2026-09-23) is superseded by the FEEDS mirror above"

- [x] **Q1921 MEDIUM A gift-card-funded job whose payment answer is lost loses its photos on the retry and the poster is told "Couldn't start payment" (flagged in the 2026-10-17 hub handoff for haul-away v2; confirmed on main 7b4eceb3f by code read, not seen on prod).** src/pages/post-job/useJobSubmit.ts: when requestEscrowCheckout fails on the wire (outcomeUnknown) the job is kept for a retry (attempt.jobId, line 467-472). If the first call had already run redeem_gift_card to 'settled', the job is now payment_status 'escrow', so the retry's fee-model stamp in supabase/functions/create-payment/index.ts matches 0 rows and throws "This job's payment has already been processed". The client reads that as a server refusal and calls cleanupOrphanJob (line 729), which runs removeJobPhotos FIRST (the job row still exists, so the job-photos DELETE policy allows it) and then a job DELETE. Result: a paid job with its photos gone and an error toast. Fix: treat an already-funded answer for this attempt's own job as success (go to /payment-success), and never remove photos before the job row is confirmed deletable. The same retry path exists for haul-away v2's gift-paid jobs. GUARD when built: a test that drives startPayment with a lost first answer and an already-funded second answer and asserts no storage remove and no jobs delete. **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no commit on origin/main names it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **DONE (builder 144 re-check 2026-11-01): built in 0c45145a13 (on origin/main) as "Q2558": a refused retry reads the job first (jobIsFunded) and a funded job goes to /payment-success; cleanupOrphanJob confirms the job is deletable BEFORE removing photos. GUARD: src/pages/post-job/haulAwayRelinkNoCleanup.test.tsx ("a job a gift card funded while the answer was lost keeps its photos and its row").**

- [x] **Q1887 LOW complete-signup's no-session one-shot check is read-then-write, so two overlapping no-session calls both pass (lh-authz-rls review of a02d0cd80, 2026-10-17; code read).** A Q1810 re-send arriving while the first request still runs, or a racing stranger, both pass the terms_accepted_at read, and the avatar is upserted to a fixed key before the UPDATE, so the loser can replace the stored photo. Fix: on the no-session path add `.is("terms_accepted_at", null)` to the profiles UPDATE and answer zero rows with "Profile already completed. Please log in."; also add guard cases for a `Bearer <anon key>` caller with getUser failing and for a non-object body (401, not 500), and correct the "with NO bio ... runs again" bullet in src/pages/auth/signup/retrySignupCompletion.ts (origin/wip/lander-rest-1017, Q1810) once both land; Q1888 is fixed for back-to-back re-sends by the one-shot rule (only this overlap remains). GUARD when built: src/test/edge/complete-signup-no-session-fields.test.ts. **PROGRESS 2026-10-17 (sec-b, branch ready/sec-b-1017-6 on -5 8533128e8): the atomic part and both guard cases are FIXED.** The no-session path now TAKES the one-shot slot with one conditional UPDATE (`terms_accepted_at = now() WHERE user_id = $1 AND terms_accepted_at IS NULL`, returning the row) right after the read check and BEFORE the photo upload or any write; zero rows answers "Profile already completed. Please log in.", so a racing call uploads and writes nothing. A holder that does not finish (any answer but 200, or a throw) RELEASES it (terms_accepted_at back to NULL, keyed on its own stamp), so the member's own retry is not locked out; the completion's UPDATE overwrites the stamp with the consent time. Residual, by design: an overlapping re-send that loses the claim is told "already finished" even if the holder then fails; the member then finishes through Log In. GUARD: src/test/edge/complete-signup-no-session-fields.test.ts, a live-row double where an UPDATE lands only if its WHERE matches at that moment (src/test/edge/mocks/completeSignupWrites.ts): Promise.allSettled of two no-session calls that both pass the read -> [200, 403], one upload, one consent row, one referral (red on 8533128e8: [200, 200]); refused-after-claim releases and the retry completes; the release is keyed on its own stamp (a completion that landed but whose answer failed is never cleared); `Bearer <anon key>` with getUser failing is the no-session path; non-object bodies (null, string, array, number) are 401 with no write. From the lh-authz-rls review of a8e881b8c (APPROVE WITH NOTES): the 18+ gate now runs BEFORE the photo upload, so a refused no-session call uploads nothing (red on a8e881b8c). 4 new @mutate killed; all 26 registered on complete-signup still killed. Not done (review note c, optional, pre-existing window): the no-session completion UPDATE could also require the claim stamp. LEFT: the "with NO bio ... runs again" bullet in src/pages/auth/signup/retrySignupCompletion.ts, which exists only on origin/wip/lander-rest-1017 (Q1810), so it cannot be edited here; correct it once Q1810 is on main (a re-send now meets "Profile already completed" whether or not a bio was saved; an overlapping one loses the claim). finding: lh-authz-rls@a02d0cd80#1; finding: lh-authz-rls@a02d0cd80#3 STATUS 2026-10-17: the batch1 commit "chore(signup): name Q1887 in the complete-signup comments" only renamed the placeholder Q1899 to Q1887 in comments; the item is unchanged (what remains is the retrySignupCompletion.ts bullet, still on origin/wip/lander-rest-1017). **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, the item's own 10-17 status says unchanged; the remaining bullet sits on origin/wip/lander-rest-1017.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **DONE 2026-11-02 (builder 156):** retrySignupCompletion.ts is on main now; its 'with NO bio ... runs again' bullet is corrected (a no-session re-send meets 'Profile already completed' with or without a bio; an overlapping one loses the one-shot claim and writes nothing). - [x] **Q1982 LOW An Apple member in Billing Grace Period is graded as lapsed (lh-money-escrow review of 54e7b92b7, 2026-10-17; pre-existing).** apple-app-store-notifications says "a grace period shows up as an expiresDate still in the future", but per Apple's App Store Server API docs the grace window is JWSRenewalInfo.gracePeriodExpiresDate and the transaction's expiresDate is not extended, so a member in grace (subscription status 4) has the tier cleared and gets it back only if the renewal goes through. Only matters if Billing Grace Period is switched on in App Store Connect (owner setting, not measured). Prod 2026-10-17: 0 Apple-anchored profiles. Fix: fetchLatestSubscriptionTransaction (_shared/appleAppStore.ts, Q1860) already receives status and signedRenewalInfo; return them, and treat status 4 with gracePeriodExpiresDate > now as entitled until gracePeriodExpiresDate. - [x] **Q1791 MEDIUM Deleting an account never revokes its Sign in with Apple token (found preparing the 1.0.4 resubmission, 2026-10-13).** Apple requires an app offering Sign in with Apple to revoke the user's Apple tokens when the account is deleted (App Store Review 5.1.1(v)). Measured 2026-10-13: no call to appleid.apple.com/auth/revoke anywhere in supabase/ or src/, including supabase/functions/delete-own-account. Fix: keep the Apple refresh token at sign-in (server side), and have delete-own-account call /auth/revoke with a client secret signed by the Sign in with Apple key. Needs that key from the owner. Code: migration 20261030050004 adds public.apple_sign_in_tokens (service role only, CASCADE); the iOS app sends the one-time Apple code to the new apple-token-store function, which trades it with Apple for the refresh token; accountPurge (run by all four delete paths before auth.admin.deleteUser) calls /auth/revoke once and drops the row, recorded as step apple_revoke but not blocking the deletion. GUARD: src/test/edge/appleSignInRevoke.test.ts (one revoke call per Apple-linked deletion, before purge_user_data; every deleteUser caller runs purgeAccount first) + src/lib/socialLogin.test.ts (iOS hands over the code). STILL NEEDED before it does anything live: the owner makes a Sign in with Apple key in the Apple developer account and sets APPLE_SIGNIN_PRIVATE_KEY + APPLE_SIGNIN_KEY_ID; until then both halves answer not_configured. Accounts that signed in with Apple before this ships have no stored token and cannot be revoked until they sign in again; web/Android Apple sign-in stores none (only iOS sends the code).** **2026-10-30 B84 (wip/b84-apple-revoke): apple-token-store now stores a token only when the id_token Apple returns from the same code exchange is for our client (iss appleid.apple.com, aud the client id) and its sub equals one of the CALLER's Apple identities (identity_data.sub; measured on prod: all 16 apple identities have it, equal to provider_id). A code from another Apple account answers 403 identity_mismatch and nothing is stored. - [x] **Q2082 HIGH Jobs fee switch (owner 2026-10-18, confirmed twice): the POSTER pays 5% of the job price + $0.70 (card cost inside it), the Helpr keeps 100%, the Declutter rule; new jobs only, behind ONE server switch the owner flips after a native build + minSupportedBuild (lane jobs-fee, plan ~/.lh-wt/handoffs-1017/jobs-fee-plan.md).** Built so far: slice 1 the server-only stamp jobs.poster_fee_flat_cents + CHECK jobs_poster_fee_model_consistent (a 0c Helpr flat stamp only with the poster stamp, whose Helpr percent is 0) + extended enforce_jobs_fee_model_lock (20261017230824); slice 2 the shared rule _shared/posterJobFee.ts + client mirror, and helprPaysNoFee so every Helpr fee read prices a poster-fee job at 0 even when its select lacks helper_fee_percent. Slice 3a (ready/jobs-fee-1017-3): the switch row jobs_poster_fee created OFF (20261017234656); create-payment escrow on the poster-fee model when the switch is on (the "Service fee" line taxed like the job's labor per the tax memo, the poster stamp with the Helpr 0/0, PaymentIntent metadata, the model in the idempotency key; Pay in 4 = Klarna + Afterpay only at 9% + 70c inside the providers' limits); a stale-client refusal (the request must carry the fee model and amounts the app showed, src/lib/jobCheckout.ts buildFeeQuote, sent by Post a Job and the quote booking screens); every create-payment Checkout Session names its payment methods (card, Link, ACH) and turns Adaptive Pricing off; the Post a Job checkout shows the fee from the same switch; a non-US card that pays any checkout pages ops (owner: Radar rule blocks them); the apply and cancel dialogs show no Helpr fee line on a poster-fee job (review #4). Gift first (ready/jobs-fee-1017-5): a gift-funded checkout is on the poster-fee model; redeem_gift_card adds the stamped fee to the cost the gift pays first (20261018031451, PGlite 3x: $50 gift on a $40 job pays $42.70, leaves $7.30), the shortfall checkout keeps the fee stamp, Pay in 4 is refused with a gift, the app quotes the same shortfall (giftFirstPosterFee.test.ts). BEFORE THE FLIP (lh-money-escrow review of gift-first, #4; #1 closed by slice 4 below): a gift-funded job in a taxable category is charged no sales tax on labor or fee (the settle never touches Stripe, the shortfall line is non-taxable, and checkoutSessionCompleted's $0-tax alert skips gift shortfalls): tax the gift path, or refuse a gift on a taxable poster-fee job. Recurring (ready/jobs-fee-1017-6): a series is on the poster-fee model too; charge-recurring-visits charges every visit its own 5% + 70c at the series' percent, taxes the fee with the labor on a taxable category (a service_fee line in the same Stripe Tax calculation), stamps the visit with the poster stamp and Helpr 0/0, and divides the visit's rate by the taxed base; a $300+ visit is parked with its fee; Pay in 4 is refused on a series, and a Pay-in-4 stamp re-asserts recurrence_days IS NULL (recurring review #4). Refunds (ready/jobs-fee-1017-7, slice 4): a poster's own cancel of a poster-fee job (create-payment cancel_escrow and the void-cancelled-payments sweep) keeps Stripe's real card fee only, never the 5% + 70c (_shared/refundAmount.ts posterCancelKeptFeeCents + client mirror, the approved wording 'less the card-processing cost'); disputes and admin refunds already keep the card cost only on a poster-fee job (cardCostFeeCents is 0 there, so Stripe's real fee is kept). A crew's unfilled spots return their whole share of the fee on a poster-fee job and keep only that share of Stripe's fee, once (process-scheduled-payouts feeHoldsCardFee); the charge.refunded fallback notice names the card processing fee, not a service fee. Gift-first review #1 closes: the gift part of the fee carries no card cost and restore_gift_card_for_job returns it whole, the card part keeps only its own Stripe fee. BEFORE THE FLIP (lh-money-escrow review of slice 4, #2 #3 #5 #6 #8): (a) DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1018-1; OWNER pop-up 2026-10-18 'J3': the tax on a kept amount is KEPT FROM THE REFUND, we never pay it): _shared/refundAmount.ts refundToPayerCents takes the charge's tax (chargeTaxCentsFor: the Checkout Session's total_details.amount_tax, since the edge's pinned API 2025-08-27.basil returns no PaymentIntent tax figure; a visit's stored tax_cents, with taxBaseCents = labor + tax so a legacy visit's untaxed card-cost fee carries none) and also keeps taxOnKeptCents = ceil(kept x tax / (captured - tax)) on everything it keeps (card fee, late-cancel fee, crew block fees, setup fee), so the tax Stripe's proportional reversal leaves collected is exactly what was kept; every pricing call passes it (cancel_escrow, admin_refund_dispute, admin_refund_general, execute-dispute-split, void-cancelled-payments, both charge-recurring-visits withholds), money-reconciliation's under-refund ceiling allows it, and the client mirror + dispute preview match; notices say it plainly ('$0.39 of that is the sales tax on what we kept, which isn't refunded'; the webhook fallback 'the card processing fee and the sales tax on what we kept'; visits 'the card processor's fee and any sales tax on it'). GUARD: src/test/refundKeepsTaxOnKeptAmounts.test.ts (a sweep proving never less than the tax left collected and at most 1c more, plus an inventory of every refundToPayerCents call that must pass taxCents; 5 @mutate RED), src/lib/refundAmount.parity.test.ts, the executed src/test/edge/create-payment.test.ts taxed admin-refund case. LEFT: Q2148 (the crew unfilled-spot refund, the late-cancel hold capture, the toast, Shops/Declutter, the stale-visit poster-fee base); the owner's 'Q1981' (a poster who cancelled while the debit was pending keeps the strike when it fails) was already made impossible by design by Q2074 (no hire before the money arrives), and src/test/failedBankPaymentKeepsCancelStrike.test.ts guards that the failed-payment path never removes a strike; (b) DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1017-8): money-reconciliation's under-refund ceiling takes posterCancelKeptFeeCents and selects poster_fee_flat_cents, so a poster-fee cancel that keeps the 5% + 70c pages (src/test/edge/money-reconciliation-stripe.test.ts, red on both @mutate lines); (c) DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1017-9): with no balance transaction read, _shared/stripeFees.ts estimatedFeeCentsFor estimates a Klarna (5.99% + 30c) or Afterpay (6% + 30c) charge at its own rate (rates read from stripe.com/pricing/local-payment-methods 2026-10-18), by the charge's method or, unexpanded, when every allowed method is Pay-in-4 (src/lib/actualOrEstimatedFeeCents.test.ts); an ACH charge with no fee read still takes the card estimate, over-keeping from the poster (review LOW, barely reachable: a succeeded ACH charge has its fee); (d) DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1017-10): the cancel toast prices the gift against src/lib/cancelRefundCopy.ts giftCostCents (budget + urgent + the stamped fee, redeem_gift_card's cost), passed by ActivityDialogs (CancellationDialog.giftToast.test.tsx, cancelRefundCopy.test.ts); (f) refundNotice's fallback (charge.refunded before the path's own notice, _shared/refundNotice.ts serviceCents 0 branch) calls a crew cancel's whole kept remainder 'the card processing fee' even when it includes block fees void-cancelled-payments withheld: subtract the crew's block fees before labelling the rest (slice-4 delta re-review #1, LOW). DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1017-11): when the words come from the job (no kept reason, no dispute or fee-withheld metadata), sendRefundNotice reads a cancelled leadless crew's crew_block_fees (the crews the sweep withholds them on) and the fallback names them on their own; a failed read sends no mislabelled notice and the webhook pages ops (src/test/edge/refundNotice.test.ts). Its review LOWs not fixed: a payee in the other Stripe mode leaves rows the sweep did not withhold unvoided (the notice over-counts; Part F would also pay them), and a member's deletion after settlement voids a row (a late notice under-counts); (e) DONE in slice 4: the cancel_escrow and sweep payment_refunds reason strings name the card processing fee on a poster-fee job (asserted in create-payment-poster-fee and void-cancelled-payments-fee-ledger tests). Gift-paid fee: a cancel restores it whole (PGlite src/test/pglite/posterFeeGiftFirst.pglite.mjs: $42.70 back on a $40 job + $2.70 fee, a half share $21.35). Still to build: the Pay-in-4 choice on the checkout screen (server side built); the job-address performance_location for tax sourcing (Stripe Checkout supports it per its docs, not yet measured on our API version); checkout-completed comparing amount_total and metadata to the stamp; engagement-automations revenue and the money-reconciliation poster-fee charge check; BEFORE THE FLIP items DONE (ready/jobs-fee-1017-4): a recurrence_days change on a poster-fee job is refused (20261018024950, PGlite 3x), the stored sales_tax_rate divides by budget + the taxed fee (_shared/salesTax.ts jobEscrowTaxableBaseCents), a TEST-mode Checkout Session with only klarna + afterpay_clearpay was created (API 2025-08-27.basil names the param payment_method_types; the 2026-09-30.preview version renamed it allowed_payment_method_types, so a Stripe API version bump must rename it), and poster_fee_flat_cents joined JOB_READABLE_COLUMN_LIST (measured live); the Helpr-fault refund (owner 10-18: a no-show or withdrawal refunds the poster in full and the Helpr owes the card cost; no job path detects Helpr fault today, so this needs a fault signal first; a ban-ended visit and a cancel after a Helpr withdrawal still keep Stripe's fee, slice-4 review #8); every display; Help Center + Terms (wording APPROVED by the owner 2026-10-18, shown only while the switch is on, Terms version bump). - [x] **Q2494 MEDIUM A Helpr delivery from a saved pickup place books from the seller's usual pickup times, not that place's days, and a buyer's item page shows only the usual times (builder 36 2026-10-30).** Measured 2026-10-30 by reading the newest definitions, not on prod (the porch places migration 20261030164712 is not live yet): a porch order books only its frozen place's weekdays (declutter_order_is_porch_day, read by declutter_porch_days and declutter_book_porch_day); a meetup books inside declutter_pickup_windows (declutter_book_pickup, declutter_slot_in_windows); helpr_task_pickup_windows also reads declutter_pickup_windows and never the place, so a Helpr can be offered Saturday at a place set to Mon, Wed, Fri. ItemPickupTimes on the item page shows the usual times to every buyer, and declutter_seller_porch_places answers only a buyer with that seller in their cart, so the client cannot show a place's days there. Both need a server change, so the lead decides. The seller's screens now say which is which (data-place-days-rule, porchPlacesUi.test.tsx).
  STALE 2026-11-01 (Auditor 11 relevance pass, closed by Builder 129): fixed by LEAD-PLACE-TIMES: helpr_task_pickup_windows now reads the place's frozen hours (migration 20261031145652_declutter_place_hours.sql). NO-GUARD: closed as stale; the code it described is gone or already fixed.
- [x] **Q1963 MEDIUM Tapping Post while on /post-job?declutterOrder= (or ?haulAway=) keeps the pre-filled form on screen but drops the link, so the job can be saved as a plain draft and posted and paid with no link to the Declutter order (lh-money-escrow + lh-silent-failure review of the lander's Declutter-train merge 9c855c737, 2026-10-17; code read, not driven).** MobileNav.tsx:657 and DesktopSidebarNav.tsx:232 navigate("/post-job"); the route is not keyed (App.tsx:245), so PostJob stays mounted. usePostJobForm recomputes noDraft and declutterOrderId from the live URL each render: noDraft turns false (the 2 s autosave in useJobFormEffects stores the Add a Helpr form as an ordinary draft) and declutterOrderId turns null (the job posts with no linkSetupJobOrTell), so a seller-fault refund of the order (declutter_refund_cancels_setup_jobs, 20261017073146) would not cancel or refund that setup job. A haul-away keeps its itemIds in state, so only its draft gate is hit. Fix: decide the intent once per mount (useState initialiser, as useHaulAwayPrefill keeps itemIds) and remount or reset the form when ?declutterOrder=/?haulAway= disappears (key the form subtree on them). GUARD when built: a usePostJobForm test that drops the param mid-mount and asserts no draft write and the order link kept. finding: lh-money-escrow@9c855c737#2 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no commit on origin/main names it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **DONE 2026-11-02 (builders 166/170): useDeclutterIntent reads ?declutterOrder= / ?haulAway= once per mount (the draft gate and the submit's order id come from that read), and PostJob's route keys the form on the link (postJobIntentKey), so tapping Post opens a fresh, unlinked post. GUARD: src/pages/post-job/declutterLinkSurvivesPost.test.tsx (3 of 5 cases red with the fix reverted; 5 @mutate lines, vacuity --only 5/5 killed).**
- [x] **Q2587 HIGH A Helpr on a no-show or a job cancelled on its day after they confirmed sees a primary "On my way".** Jobs audit 1102b (~/.lh-shots/audit-1102b-findings.md, main 9da4a32d35 + the B111 job stage preview, 11-02) row 1: under "This job was cancelled" / "Not paid" the Helpr gets olive "On my way" beside Message. Cause: src/pages/jobs/jobPage/hiredHelprFace.ts builds the status line with the application hard-coded accepted, and JobPage.tsx does not gate on job.status. Fix: no step on a cancelled/expired/closed job or a closed application. Fixed by Builder 168: hiredHelprFace takes the Helpr's own application and gives no step on a cancelled/expired job or a closed application; guard src/test/closedJobNoHelprStep.test.ts.
- [x] **Q2589 MEDIUM "On the way · since 2:49 AM" sits above "21h 0m until the job starts".** Jobs audit 1102b (~/.lh-shots/audit-1102b-findings.md, main 9da4a32d35 + the B111 job stage preview, 11-02) row 3: the start countdown keeps running after the Helpr sets out, and for a no-start-time job it counts to midnight. Hide the start countdown once on the way; a no-time job says "Today". - [x] **Q2320 LOW If the signed-in account changes while the Notifications screen stays open, its switches stay live with the previous account's settings, and one press saves them into the new account (lh-silent-failure review of ready/shell4-notif-load-fail, 2026-10-21, note 4; existed before that branch).** src/components/NotificationPreferences.tsx: the load effect re-runs on a new authUserId and sets userId at once, but never resets `loaded`, `prefs` or the column flags, so while the new account's read is in flight (and for good when it has no saved row) the whole-row upsert writes the old account's values. ## Archived 2026-11-02 — from "QUEUE (cont.) — carried; the retired audit-bus section's table (2026-09-23) is superseded by the FEEDS mirror above"

- [x] **Q2343 MEDIUM The Posted tab says "All · 213" for poster-e2e while the database holds 436 of its jobs, and 4 of its open seed jobs appear in no section.** Seen 2026-10-21 by the lead. Find which jobs the tab's count and sections drop, and why. **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **CLOSED 2026-11-02 by design (Builder 189, measured):** the gap is the never-paid rows, which the owner ruled are not posts (2026-09-21 "a job can never be posted if it was never paid for"; 2026-09-27 "Unpaid jobs should not show in post anywhere. Even hidden."). useActivityFilters drops every job whose payment_status is unpaid / abandoned / failed (jobIsUnfundedDraft, src/lib/neverPaidStatuses.ts) BEFORE both the tab counts and the lists, so "All · N" = jobs minus never-paid, and every remaining job lands in exactly one of the five groups (ActivityBucketGroups files every bucket). Read-only prod SQL 2026-11-02 (jobs where customer_id = poster-e2e 951264df, grouped by status, payment_status): 421 jobs; never-paid 91 (cancelled+unpaid 51, cancelled+abandoned 34, open+abandoned 3, open+unpaid 2, pending_approval+unpaid 1), so All should read 330. The "open seed jobs in no section" are the open never-paid rows (5 today: 3 abandoned, 2 unpaid), hidden by that same rule. Code-read residue, not measured on screen: a cancelled card the poster bulk-dismisses (useBulkDismiss, sessionStorage, this session only) leaves the list but still counts in the "All" / "Cancelled" chip counts, which are tallied upstream of PostedJobsTab's visibleJobs filter. NO-GUARD: nothing changed; the never-paid rule it relies on is guarded by src/test/unpaidJobsNeverShowInPost.test.ts.
- [x] **Q2443 HIGH MUST before the next App Store build, and before ready/card-hold-on-1027 lands: an app build without src/lib/cardHoldCapture.ts cannot complete a hire on a held card (Q2399; finding: lh-money-escrow@b67262b5b5#1).** Read 2026-10-27 (card-hold-dark lane, code read only, nothing run live): in 20261027185146 accept_job_offer, respond_to_direct_offer and rpc_group_member_confirm RAISE payment_not_captured for a READY Helpr on a 'held' job, and only the new web code (src/lib/cardHoldCapture.ts withCardHoldCapture) answers that by calling create-payment capture_hold. A build already in the App Store has neither: its Accept calls accept_job_offer directly, has no copy for payment_not_captured (origin/main src/lib/lifecycleErrors.ts RPC_ERROR_COPY has no such key) and shows "Couldn't accept the job — please try again." (origin/main src/components/job-card/activityActions/useOfferHandlers.ts accept path), and because the RAISE rolls the call back no job_accept_pending / crew_confirm_pending row is written, so card-hold-sweep (completePendingAccept / completePendingCrewConfirms, which already capture and complete a pending accept once the Helpr is ready) never finishes it. Unless the Helpr accepts again from an up-to-date build (the web app), the offer can run out its response_deadline unanswered. FIX (server fallback, not built: it is not contained to those three functions): for a ready Helpr on a held job, record the pending row and return a success-pending answer instead of raising, so the sweep charges and completes the hire within one 10-minute run; create-payment capture_hold must then accept that answer as its proof that every gate passed (today it requires the payment_not_captured refusal), cardHoldCapture.ts must call capture_hold on it so new builds still hire at once, the crew answer needs a shape an old build's confirmCrewSpot does not throw on (it calls stampOrThrow on helper_confirmed_at unless action is pending_setup), and the PGlite proof (H5, H10, H12), src/test/edge/card-hold.test.ts and e2e/journeys/05-card-hold.spec.ts move with it; it needs its own lh-money-escrow pass. Done when: an accept_job_offer call with no capture_hold on a held job, as a ready Helpr, ends hired after one card-hold-sweep run (PGlite + edge test), red on 20261027185146 as it stands. STATUS 2026-10-27 (lead ruling, at the flip): no App Store version has ever been live and no member has an installed build, so no public client can hit this; for the flip it means only that the next App Store build must include src/lib/cardHoldCapture.ts, which every build cut from main now does. Stays open until that iOS release ships. **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **CLOSED 2026-11-02 (Builder 189, lead order; measured):** the TestFlight build's commit 34137943790f (ios-beta run created 2026-11-02 15:05Z, `gh run list --workflow ios-beta.yml`, status queued when read) holds src/lib/cardHoldCapture.ts (`git ls-tree 3413794`); withCardHoldCapture wraps accept_job_offer and respond_to_direct_offer in useOfferHandlers.ts (:382, :520), InstantBookingCard.tsx (:53) and crewLifecycle.ts, answering payment_not_captured with create-payment capture_hold; lifecycleErrors.ts has payment_not_captured copy (3 tables). Older builds are forced to update: CLIENT_COMPAT_EPOCH = 3 in src/lib/clientCompat.ts at that commit, and live prod `select public.client_compat_floor()` = 3 with migration 20261102082018 applied (read-only SQL 2026-11-02), so a build without the capture code cannot reach the hire.
  NOTE 2026-11-01 (Auditor 10 triage): the fix is on main; leave this unticked until the next iOS build ships with it.

The Q1596 fix (1f691e3c0f, on the b182+ chain) drops denials only AFTER ProtectedRoute marks the loss, but the denial that TRIGGERS the check (and any others in flight while confirmSessionLost runs its refresh) is reported first, so this case still reports. Proposal: report() holds a grant denial (isPermissionDenied) for a short window; markSessionLost() drops the held ones; a "not lost" answer or the window ending reports them. That changes report() timing for every denial (errorLogger.test, errorLoggerPostgrestFields, errorLoggerSessionLossNoise, staleClient tests), so the lead decides first. **DONE 2026-11-03 (auto-tick, verified live): every done-when marker on this item read as expected on prod (got closed, want closed).**

- [x] **Q1755 MEDIUM A test account can apply to (and be hired on) a REAL job, which puts a test account on real money (found by the lh-authz-rls and lh-money-escrow reviews of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12; the 2026-10-09 incident was exactly this: the shared test Helpr applied to the owner's real job).** enforce_application_job_state rule C6 only stops a REAL user applying to a seed job (`v_job.is_seed AND seed_hidden_for(helper)`); nothing stops the reverse, nor a direct offer or accept between a real poster and a test account. With test mode for test accounts the money side is safe (a payout of live escrow to a test Helpr refuses as mixed_parties, and the poster's refund still runs in live mode), but such a job can never be paid out and needs a refund. Fix at the source: refuse a test account applying to, being offered or accepting a non-seed job (and a real account on a seed job, as today), in the trigger and the offer/accept RPCs. Data-model change: migration, PGlite 3x, lh-authz-rls review. Its name sorts after trg_jobs_insert_column_lock and trg_jobs_seed_from_poster, so on INSERT it reads the final is_seed. Measured on prod first (read-only): 63 test accounts, all is_seed profiles; 461 jobs posted by test accounts, all is_seed; 0 applications, hires or direct offers pairing a test account with a non-seed job, so nothing existing is refused. A real member on a seed job keeps today's rule (C6, unchanged; conservative). GUARD: src/test/testAccountsOffRealJobs.test.ts (the three tables carry the trigger; it fires after every is_seed setter, inventory from source) + src/test/pglite/testAccountsOffRealJobs.pglite.mjs (16 PASS applied 3x; 6 FAIL with NEW_MIGRATION=skip). - [x] **Q2418 LOW Declutter haul-away sweep: a retry of an abandoned haul-away job that re-linked just before the daily sweep can still be paid after the sweep put its items back on sale (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1962 sweep, 2026-10-27, #1; code read, not seen on prod).** declutter_link_haul_away's same-job re-link writes nothing (no jobs.updated_at, no link updated_at), so this order is possible: Post a Job's retry re-links an 'abandoned' job (useJobSubmit.ts, the attempt.jobId branch), the 15:21 UTC declutter_haul_away_sweep (20261027165903, Part C) then undoes the 7-day-old link and puts the item back on sale, then create-payment re-mints a checkout for the job ('abandoned' is re-mintable) and the seller pays for a haul-away whose item is on sale. Needs a tab left open 7+ days with the in-memory attempt and a press landing during the run. Fix: when Part C undoes an open 'abandoned' job's link, record the job id (a tombstone), and have create-payment's escrow path refuse a tombstoned job unless a haul-away link to it exists again; or have the same-job re-link touch the link's updated_at. - [x] **Q1978 MEDIUM A late bank-payment failure that lands while a payout or dispute split is already moving money un-escrows a job whose Helpr is being paid (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1970 CI fix, 2026-10-17; code read).** process-scheduled-payouts and release-payout send the Stripe transfer first and flip the job to 'released' after; execute-dispute-split keeps payment_status 'escrow' under its own lock. A payment_intent.payment_failed in that window still matches unfundFailedEscrow's compare-and-set (payment_status IN escrow/payout_pending), so the job reads 'failed', the Helpr is told the payout is on hold (untrue) and ops gets two critical pages. - [x] **Q1992 MEDIUM A crew refund's dedupe misses a ledger row it did not write itself, so after Stripe's 24-hour idempotency window a second real refund can go out (lh-money-escrow review of 52ba80829, #2; pre-existing; code read 2026-10-17, not measured).** process-scheduled-payouts refundUnfilledCrewShares finds its earlier refund by a payment_refunds row with the same job_id AND source (crew_unfilled_refund / crew_dispute_refund). Two ways that row is missing: (a) charge.refunded (stripe-webhook/handlers/chargeRefunded.ts l.339-350) books the same refund first, with job_id and source stripe_dashboard, and the crew upsert (onConflict stripe_refund_id, ignoreDuplicates) leaves that label; (b) the run dies between stripe.refunds.create and the ledger upsert. The next run then calls refunds.create again under the same idempotency key: within 24h Stripe returns the same refund (same amount) or refuses a changed amount; after 24h the key has expired and a SECOND real refund goes out. Prod: 0 crew refunds and 0 completed group jobs (2026-10-17), so nothing has happened yet. Fix: stamp the crew refund itself first (refunds.create sends no metadata today, index.ts ~l.842, so no Stripe refund says it is a crew one: add metadata { job_id, crew_source }); then, before refunding, also look for a live refund on the charge stamped with this job's crew source (stripe.refunds.list({ payment_intent })) or a payment_refunds row on the same stripe_payment_intent_id of source stripe_dashboard, and relabel a stripe_dashboard row to the crew source, as recordRefund does (Q1261 (6)) (lh-money-escrow delta review of 45ccc2384, #5). GUARD when built: a process-scheduled-payouts-crew case where the prior row is stripe_dashboard (and one with no row but a live stamped Stripe refund) that sends no second refund. finding: lh-money-escrow@52ba80829#2 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no commit on origin/main names it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **stale-sweep 11-02: fixed by b8b297affe (crew refund dedupe finds a live stamped Stripe refund or a stripe_dashboard row; Sensitive-Review lh-silent-failure approve), guard src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts-crew.test.ts**

- [x] **Q2172 MEDIUM restore_helpr_fault_fee edge cases in the payout deduction (jobs-fee lane, 2026-10-19, lh-money-escrow review of c4ec33afb #5).** (a) release-payout's 'conflict' exit (Stripe has a transfer, no ledger row) restores the deduction although money may have moved with the fee held back, so the debt can be taken twice; (b) restore never runs for a (job, Helpr) with an older reversed row, so a later failed attempt loses the deduction; (c) an adopted stuck pending claim after a bill landed in between reads a deduction as paid that was never withheld; (d) a retry whose cap shrank (onboarding fee owed on the second run) gets the first, larger answer and the TS guard refuses forever (stuck payout). Also the open race from the fork: a run stopping before its claim can restore while another run's claim is being inserted. Re-review of b1730c932 sighted two of these again: #3 (release-payout and process-scheduled-payouts race; one run restores before its claim while the other already re-read the deduction, so the Helpr is withheld X now and charged X again; fix: take the debt after claimPayout) and #5 (= (d): clamp a repeat answer to the current cap and give back the excess). - [x] **Q2115 LOW first_payment_collected is not sent when the checkout return beats the Stripe webhook, or when the first job was paid without a payment intent (lh-silent-failure review of the sec-c train, 2026-10-15).** Reviewer note on src/pages/post-job/PaymentSuccess.tsx (fireReturnEvents): the first-payment check counts the poster's jobs with a non-null stripe_payment_intent_id, which only the webhook writes (supabase/functions/stripe-webhook checkoutSessionCompleted.ts), but fireReturnEvents runs on the first poster-confirmed read, often while payment_status is still unpaid (the case the 4 x 1.5 s poll exists for); then this job is not yet in the paid set and the event is silently not sent (the old `count <= 1` check did send it). Second gap, NOT measured: the reviewer's read-only prod query found 7 paid jobs (escrow 6, released 1) with a null payment intent against 10 with one; whether those are gift-covered settles or seeds was not checked, and if gift-covered, a gift-funded first job never sends the event. Analytics under-count only: no payment, escrow or navigation behaviour depends on it. Fix idea: fire first_payment_collected once the poll reads a HELD status, counting the poster's OTHER paid jobs (by payment_status, not the intent id) as zero. GUARD when built: a case in src/test/keyEventsFire.test.tsx where the first read is unpaid and a later poll is escrow. finding: lh-silent-failure@1e0c79b08#4 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, the commit naming it only numbered it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **DONE 2026-11-02 (Builder 13): PaymentSuccess fires first_payment_collected once, only after its poll READS this job as paid (escrow / payout_pending / released; a card hold is not a collection), and trackFirstPaymentCollected counts the poster's OTHER jobs by payment_status (escrow, payout_pending, released, refunded, chargeback, cancelling), never the webhook-only intent id, so a gift-covered first job counts too. Analytics only. GUARD: src/test/keyEventsFire.test.tsx "the first read is unpaid and a later poll reads escrow..." and siblings (2 red on main's sources; 5 @mutate red by hand).**

- [x] **Q2071 MEDIUM A released recurring date's pending $300+ visit Checkout stays payable (lh-money-escrow review of Q1802, 2026-10-17).** No path that releases a date (series_release_dates, give_up_series_dates, helper_cancel_booking, end_recurring_series; live bodies read in pg_proc) touches recurring_visit_payments, and the charge-recurring-visits sweep closes a still-pending row only on its visit date or once the series is over. create-payment's recurring_visit action and stripe-webhook's settleRecurringVisitCheckout (handlers/settleRecurringVisitCheckout.ts:85-95) never check that the date is still held, so the poster can pay for a date nobody holds, and the visit-date sweep then refunds it less the card fee: a fee lost to our own release. - [x] **Q1756 LOW Re-minting a test account's checkout leaves its old LIVE Checkout Session open until it expires (found by the lh-money-escrow review of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12).** create-payment escrow skips reading a cs_live_ session on a job that now runs in test mode (the test key cannot read it) and mints a cs_test_ one, but does not expire the live one, which Stripe keeps payable for up to 24 hours. Measured 2026-10-12 by the reviewer: 0 of the 68 abandoned and 2 unpaid cs_live_ sessions on test accounts' jobs were ever paid; after deploy no new live session is minted for a test account. Expiring it needs the LIVE key on a test account's request (the one live call this design avoids); decide whether that is worth it. finding: lh-money-escrow@7389f2a30#6 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no change to create-payment for this; it is a decide-if-worth-it LOW (0 such sessions ever paid).** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **RE-MEASURED 2026-11-03 5:57 PM (Builder 81, read-only SQL on prod):** of the jobs owned by public.test_accounts, 0 carry a cs_live_ stripe_session_id (534 carry cs_test_), so there is no live session left that a re-mint could strand, and none since the 10-12 deploy. Unless a test account gets a live session again, the case cannot happen; the lead may close it as not worth the live-key call. **DONE 2026-11-03 (lead lexilombas-01 5:59 PM, on Builder 81's measurement):** closed as not worth the live-key call; proof: 0 cs_live_ sessions on test accounts' jobs (534 cs_test_), read-only SQL 5:57 PM. NO-GUARD: nothing is built to recur; test accounts no longer get live Checkout Sessions since the 10-12 test-mode deploy, and a re-mint has nothing to strand without one.
- [x] **Q1913 LOW A crew with no PaymentIntent and no gift still pays its crew block fees from the platform's own money (lh-money-escrow review of Q1806, 2026-10-15; code read, 0 gift_cards with a job_id on prod).** void-cancelled-payments' no-payment branch calls payCrewBlockFees(..., null) whenever a block fee is owed, and Part F pays any left owed on the settled job, without checking that a gift paid for the job (Q1490 behaviour; Q1806 now checks this for the late-cancellation fee only). Reachable by a seed crew in escrow with no PaymentIntent, or a gift an earlier run gave back whole. Fix: read giftFeeFunding when block fees are owed too; with no money behind the job, void the rows (or leave them for a person) and alert instead of paying. Guard: src/test/edge/void-cancelled-payments-crew.test.ts.**
- [x] **Q1904 LOW A cancel that runs in the seconds between a checkout funding its job and settleOnboardingFee refunding a duplicate $2 setup fee returns those $2 twice (lh-money-escrow review of ba10093ae, Q1675 follow-up; code read, narrow window, not seen on prod).** checkoutSessionCompleted sets payment_status 'escrow' (~958), calls instant-job-match, and only then runs settleOnboardingFee (~1227); a cancel_escrow or void-cancelled-payments in that gap refunds the full ceiling (which still holds the duplicate $2), then settleOnboardingFee refunds the $2 again out of the card fee. Fix: settleOnboardingFee skips its refund (and says so) when the charge's amount_refunded already covers the escrow ceiling, or the duplicate refund runs before the escrow flip. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge test with a charge already refunded by a cancel. finding: lh-money-escrow@ba10093ae#3 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **STATUS 2026-11-03 (Builder 64, read from main at deployed f8ac218e50; migrations confirmed in prod schema_migrations by read-only SELECT): DONE: 557760c914 (settleOnboardingFee skips its refund when a cancel already returned the duplicate $2). Guard: src/test/edge/dupSetupFeeAfterCancel.test.ts.**
- [x] **Q1908 LOW A held tip or won-chargeback re-pay decides its Stripe mode from today's test-account enrolment, not from where the money sits (lh-money-escrow review of Q1753, 2026-10-15).** For a job with no cs_ prefix (gift-funded), an account enrolled or un-enrolled between the charge and the re-drive sends transfers.create to the other key; Stripe refuses it as a non-transient error, so the row goes 'failed' with a critical "pay by hand" page. No money is lost; someone is paged wrongly. Measured 2026-10-15: 0 open tip_hold_redrives rows and 0 open held chargeback_clawbacks rows. Fix: store the event's livemode on the row when the webhook writes it (a nullable stripe_mode column), re-drive on that mode (still checked against the payees), and fall back to clientForJob only when it is null. Done when an edge test with a stored mode that differs from today's enrolment pins it. finding: lh-money-escrow@886256a04#1 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no commit on origin/main names it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **BUILT 2026-11-03 (Builder 50, wip/b50-money): migration 20261103112413 adds a nullable stripe_mode (live/test) to tip_hold_redrives and chargeback_clawbacks; the webhook writes it from PaymentIntent/Dispute livemode; re-drives run on it via clientForStoredMode (payees still checked), NULL falls back to clientForJob; no backfill (0 rows on prod 11-03). Guard: src/test/edge/cronStripeModePerJob.test.ts "Q1908"; PGlite src/test/pglite/redriveRowsStoreStripeMode.pglite.mjs.** **STATUS 2026-11-03 (Builder 64, read from main at deployed f8ac218e50; migrations confirmed in prod schema_migrations by read-only SELECT): DONE: cdecc30003 (stripe_mode stored on tip_hold_redrives / chargeback_clawbacks, live on prod; re-drives use it).**
- [x] **Q1926 LOW On a late in-time Declutter payment (B41) a SELLER who blocked the buyer reads sold_elsewhere, so the platform pays the card fee, where the same block on an open checkout reads seller_backed_out and the seller pays (lh-money-escrow review of 905501cb2, finding 1, the part older than that commit; code read).** declutter_mark_order_paid path (b) in 20261017060455 (from 20261014224914) folds the seller's block into v_sale_ok and answers sold_elsewhere; path (c) picks seller_backed_out first. The buyer is refunded in full either way; only who absorbs the fee differs. The buyer-block half of the finding is fixed in the stack (seller_out judged on the items alone). Fix: pick (b)'s reason with (c)'s CASE, and decide the buyer-blocked case with the owner (a buyer_cancelled refund keeps the fee, which (b)'s "every cent" notice would then contradict). Same question for a BUYER who blocked a seller who is NOT out: (b) reads sold_elsewhere (platform pays), (c) reads buyer_cancelled (the card fee is kept) (same review, re-review note 2). Migration 20261103095253 makes path (b) read seller_backed_out (the seller pays the card fee) when the seller blocked the buyer and the items alone would still let the sale stand; proof src/test/pglite/declutterLatePaymentSellerBlock.pglite.mjs (skip red). The BUYER-blocked half waits on the owner (still order_closed, we pay the fee); stays [ ] until that is answered.** **STATUS 2026-11-03 (Builder 64, read from main at deployed f8ac218e50; migrations confirmed in prod schema_migrations by read-only SELECT): Seller half DONE: 09b8559ead (migration 20261103095253 live). - [x] **Q1980 LOW A redelivered completion of a bank checkout whose payment failed, with no gift, still stamps its dead PaymentIntent on the job (lh-money-escrow review of b837455eb, #1 follow-up; code read).** Q1970 stops the gift-shortfall case, but a plain bank checkout D1 whose first delivery threw is retried for days; if its debit bounced and the poster opened D2, D1's retry still runs the job write (checkoutSessionCompleted, the funding CAS accepts unpaid/failed/abandoned) and stamps D1's PaymentIntent over D2's. paymentIntentSucceeded finds the job only by stripe_payment_intent_id, so D2's settled debit would find no job and be acked; if D2 was a card checkout already in escrow, the zero-row branch refunds D1's uncharged PaymentIntent and throws on every retry. Needs a failed first delivery, so rare; prod 2026-10-17: 0 unpaid/failed/abandoned jobs hold a PaymentIntent. Fix: the same stale-bank check (session not the job's current one + PaymentIntent failed) before the job write for every job checkout, returning early. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook case for a non-gift stale bounced bank completion asserting no jobs write. finding: lh-money-escrow@b837455eb#1 **TRIAGE 2026-10-21: still applies, no commit on origin/main names it; not built.** **TRIAGE 2026-10-30: still applies.** **BUILT 2026-11-03 (Builder 65, wip/b65-low, not landed; in use: ACH is in JOB_CHECKOUT_PAYMENT_METHOD_TYPES for every job checkout): checkoutSessionCompleted runs the stale bounced-bank check for every job checkout (not a tip or boost, the job write's own scope), not only a gift shortfall. A checkout that is not the job's current one (another session stamped, or none) and whose PaymentIntent is requires_payment_method or canceled returns before the job write. A processing debit, the current checkout and a paid card checkout are unchanged. Guard: src/test/edge/stripe-webhook.test.ts "a retried completion of a bounced plain bank checkout (no gift)" (2 cases red on the old handler; 2 new @mutate lines checked by hand, each killed).** **DONE 2026-11-03 (train 7, Lander 57): the built code lands in train 7 (l57-train7 on b9efb078da).**

- [x] **Q2098 LOW A late-cancelled CREW job paid partly by gift and partly by card whose gift can never pay still stays in escrow for good (the crew half of the Q1806 Option A owner rule, sec-a 2026-10-18; code read).** Q1806's Option A (owner 2026-10-18: the Helpr gets the card-covered part of the fee, we never absorb) is built for single-Helpr jobs only: giftCoversShortfall returns uncollectable only with no crewShares and no block fees, so a crew whose donation was reversed (or whose gift was already given back whole) still fails closed, with the job left in escrow, a money_at_risk page once a day and, on an uncaptured hold, an authorisation that lapses after about 7 days.
